- **ntfy Self-Hosted Push Notification Server**: ntfy is an open-source HTTP-based pub-sub notification service that lets you send push notifications to phones and desktops via simple PUT or POST requests. Self-hostable with zero signup required, it provides a dead-simple API for alerting from scripts, CI pipelines, cron jobs, and IoT devices.
